## Surprising Connections (you probably didn't know these)
- `createOpencode()` --calls--> `createOpencodeClient()`  [EXTRACTED]
  github/index.ts → packages/sdk/js/src/client.ts
- `{ use: useCommand, provider: CommandProvider }` --indirect_call--> `register()`  [INFERRED]
  packages/app/src/context/command.tsx → packages/opencode/src/server/routes/instance/httpapi/websocket-tracker.ts
- `main()` --calls--> `createOpencode()`  [EXTRACTED]
  script/duplicate-pr.ts → packages/sdk/js/src/index.ts
- `createKeys()` --calls--> `createBindingLookup()`  [EXTRACTED]
  .opencode/plugins/tui-smoke.tsx → packages/plugin/src/tui.ts
- `sessionHref()` --calls--> `base64Encode()`  [EXTRACTED]
  packages/app/e2e/regression/review-state-persistence.spec.ts → packages/core/src/util/encode.ts

- 2-file cycle: `packages/stats/app/src/i18n.ts -> packages/stats/app/src/i18n/ja.ts -> packages/stats/app/src/i18n.ts`
- 2-file cycle: `packages/stats/app/src/i18n.ts -> packages/stats/app/src/i18n/ko.ts -> packages/stats/app/src/i18n.ts`
